We present HarnessAPI, a Python framework that eliminates this duplication by treating a typed skill folder as the single source of truth. From one handler.py plus Pydantic schemas, the framework automatically derives a streaming HTTP endpoint with Server-Sent Events, an interactive OpenAPI/Swagger UI, and a zero-configuration MCP tool, all served from a single process. Dual-mode content negotiation lets the same handler serve SSE-streaming and JSON-returning clients with no handler changes. A dynamic code-generation mechanism ensures Pydantic type annotations propagate correctly to FastMCP's inspection layer, resolving a technical limitation that prevents naive closure-based registration. Measured across six representative skills using cloc, HarnessAPI reduces framework-facing boilerplate by 74% compared with a manually maintained dual-stack implementation (FastAPI server + FastMCP server). HarnessAPI subclasses FastAPI, inheriting its full middleware, dependency-injection, and deployment ecosystem. It is available at https://github.com/edwinjosechittilappilly/harnessapi and on PyPI (pip install harnessapi)

</details>


### [252] [Towards a General Intelligence and Interface for Wearable Health Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22759)
*Girish Narayanswamy,Maxwell A. Xu,A. Ali Heydari,Samy Abdel-Ghaffar,Marius Guerard,Kara Vaillancourt,Zhihan Zhang,Jake Garrison,Levi Albuquerque,Dimitris Spathis,Hong Yu,Hamid Palangi,Xuhai "Orson" Xu,David G. T. Barrett,Joseph Breda,Jed McGiffin,Yubin Kim,Yuwei Zhang,Naghmeh Rezaei,Samuel Solomon,Karan Ahuja,Tim Althoff,Jake Sunshine,Ming-Zher Poh,Benjamin Yetton,Ari Winbush,Nicholas B. Allen,James M. Rehg,Isaac Galatzer-Levy,Yun Liu,John Hernandez,Anupam Pathak,Conor Heneghan,Yuzhe Yang,Ahmed A. Metwally,Pushmeet Kohli,Mark Malhotra,Shwetak Patel,Xin Liu,Daniel McDuff*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于超过1万亿分钟、500万参与者未标注可穿戴传感器数据的预训练基础模型，通过联合缩放模型容量与数据量，在35项健康预测任务上实现系统性能提升；并利用LLM智能体自动搜索下游预测头，集成到个人健康智能体中提升临床相关性、上下文感知与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴传感器数据虽丰富，但将其转化为个性化健康洞察面临两大挑战：一是高表型多样性与个体基线差异导致低层传感器数据难以表征高层健康状态；二是配对健康标注数据的采集昂贵且回顾性标注不可行，导致高质量标注数据稀缺。

Method: 1) 在超大规模未标注数据（1万亿+分钟，500万参与者）上预训练基础模型；2) 联合缩放模型容量与预训练数据量；3) 部署LLM智能体教室自动搜索下游预测头；4) 构建个人健康智能体整合下游预测器以增强响应相关性、上下文感知与安全性。

Result: 在涵盖心血管、代谢、睡眠、心理健康、生活方式及人口统计因素的35项健康预测任务上，模型性能随数据与模型容量缩放而系统提升；实现标签高效的小样本学习与鲁棒的日常指标估计；LLM智能体带来的性能提升随LLM容量增加而增强；通过1,860份临床医生评分验证了个人健康智能体的有效性。

Conclusion: 大规模预训练与模型缩放可有效克服可穿戴健康数据标注稀缺问题，结合LLM智能体自动搜索与个人健康智能体架构，能够生成更安全、上下文更相关的个性化健康见解。

Abstract: While ubiquitous wearable sensors capture a wealth of behavioral and physiological information, effectively transforming these signals into personalized health insights is challenging. Specifically, converting low-level sensor data into representations capable of characterizing higher-level states is difficult due to high phenotypic diversity and variation in individual baseline health, physiology, and lifestyle factors. Moreover, collecting wearable data paired with health outcome annotations is laborious and expensive, and retrospective annotation remains practically unfeasible, contributing to a scarcity of data with high-quality labels. To overcome these limitations, we propose a foundation model for wearable health that is pretrained on more than one trillion minutes of unlabeled sensor signals drawn from a large cohort of five million participants. We demonstrate that the joint scaling of model capacity and pretraining data volume leads to systematic improvements in performance, as evaluated on a diverse set of 35 health prediction tasks, spanning cardiovascular, metabolic, sleep, and mental health, as well as lifestyle choices and demographic factors. We find that this population scale representation unlocks label-efficient few-shot learning and generative capabilities for robust daily metric estimation. To further leverage this learned representation, we deploy a classroom of LLM agents to autonomously search the space of downstream predictive heads built on the model embeddings, showing broad performance improvements that increase with LLM model capacity. Finally, we show how integrating these downstream predictors into a Personal Health Agent can support model responses that are more relevant, contextually aware, and safe, and we validate this via 1,860 ratings from a cohort of clinicians.

</details>


### [253] [Advancing Mathematics Research with AI-Driven Formal Proof Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22763)
*George Tsoukalas,Anton Kovsharov,Sergey Shirobokov,Anja Surina,Moritz Firsching,Gergely Bérczi,Francisco J. R. Ruiz,Arun Suggala,Adam Zsolt Wagner,Eric Wieser,Lei Yu,Aja Huang,Miklós Z. Horváth,Andrew Ferrauiolo,Henryk Michalewski,Codrut Grosu,Thomas Hubert,Matej Balog,Pushmeet Kohli,Swarat Chaudhuri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLMs结合Lean形式化验证，以每个问题几百美元的成本，自主解决了353个开放Erdős问题中的9个及492个OEIS猜想中的44个，并在组合学、优化等多个数学研究领域得到应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在数学推理中日趋强大但不可靠，限制了其在数学研究中的应用。通过让LLMs生成Lean等语言的形式化证明，有望利用形式化验证的严格性弥补LLM的不确定性，从而解决开放数学问题。

Method: 采用智能体方法，交替进行基于LLM的证明生成与基于Lean的验证；对比了最强大的智能体与基础智能体（仅交替LLM生成与Lean验证）的性能差异。

Result: 最强大的智能体自主解决了353个开放Erdős问题中的9个、492个OEIS猜想中的44个，每个问题成本仅几百美元；基础智能体在Erdős问题上复制了成功但解决最难题时成本更高。

Conclusion: AI辅助的形式化证明搜索是解决开放数学问题的有效方法，不同智能体设计对成本和能力有显著影响，该方法已实际部署于多个数学研究领域。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly excel at mathematical reasoning, but their unreliability limits their utility in mathematics research. A mitigation is using LLMs to generate formal proofs in languages like Lean. We perform the first large-scale evaluation of this method's ability to solve open problems. Our most capable agent autonomously resolved 9 of 353 open Erdős problems at the per-problem cost of a few hundred dollars, proved 44/492 OEIS conjectures, and is being deployed in combinatorics, optimization, graph theory, algebraic geometry, and quantum optics research. A basic agent alternating LLM-based generation with Lean-based verification replicated the Erdős successes but proved costlier on the hardest problems. These findings demonstrate the power of AI-aided formal proof search and shed light on the agent designs that enable it.

</details>


### [254] [LCGuard: Latent Communication Guard for Safe KV Sharing in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22786)
*Sadia Asif,Mohammad Mohammadi Amiri,Momin Abbas,Prasanna Sattigeri,Karthikeyan Natesan Ramamurthy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LCGuard框架，通过在共享KV缓存中学习表征级变换，在保持任务性能的同时减少多智能体LLM系统中的敏感信息泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的多智能体系统通过KV缓存进行潜在通信虽然高效且能保留丰富任务信息，但KV缓存编码了上下文输入、中间推理状态和智能体特定信息，形成不透明通道，使得敏感内容可能在不经文本披露的情况下在智能体间传播，存在隐私泄露风险。

Method: LCGuard将共享KV缓存视为潜在工作记忆，在缓存工件跨智能体传输前学习表征级变换。通过对抗训练形式化敏感信息泄露问题：攻击者学习重建敏感输入，LCGuard学习保持任务相关语义同时减少可重建信息量的变换。

Result: 在多种模型族和多智能体基准测试上的实验表明，LCGuard持续降低基于重建的泄露和攻击成功率，同时与标准KV共享基线相比保持有竞争力的任务性能。

Conclusion: LCGuard通过表征级变换有效平衡了KV缓存通信的效率与安全性，为基于潜在通信的多智能体系统提供了实用的隐私保护方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems increasingly rely on intermediate communication to coordinate complex tasks. While most existing systems communicate through natural language, recent work shows that latent communication, particularly through transformer key-value (KV) caches, can improve efficiency and preserve richer task-relevant information. However, KV caches also encode contextual inputs, intermediate reasoning states, and agent-specific information, creating an opaque channel through which sensitive content may propagate across agents without explicit textual disclosure. To address this, we introduce \textbf{LCGuard} (Latent Communication Guard), a framework for safe KV-based latent communication in multi-agent LLM systems. LCGuard treats shared KV caches as latent working memory and learns representation-level transformations before cache artifacts are transmitted across agents. We formalize representation-level sensitive information leakage operationally through reconstruction: a shared cache artifact is unsafe if an adversarial decoder can recover agent-specific sensitive inputs from it. This leads to an adversarial training formulation in which the adversary learns to reconstruct sensitive inputs, while LCGuard learns transformations that preserve task-relevant semantics and reduce reconstructable information. Empirical evaluations across multiple model families and multi-agent benchmarks show that LCGuard consistently reduces reconstruction-based leakage and attack success rates while maintaining competitive task performance compared to standard KV-sharing baselines.

</details>


### [255] [Gated DeltaNet-2: Decoupling Erase and Write in Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22791)
*Ali Hatamizadeh,Yejin Choi,Jan Kautz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Gated DeltaNet-2 提出一种门控增量规则，通过分离擦除门（erase gate）和写入门（write gate）来解耦线性注意力中的记忆编辑操作，在语言建模、推理和长上下文检索任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性注意力模型（如Gated DeltaNet、KDA）在压缩记忆编辑时，使用单一标量门控同时控制键侧的擦除和值侧的写入，导致两者耦合，限制了模型对记忆更新的精细控制。作者旨在解耦这两个操作以提升模型性能。

Method: 提出Gated DeltaNet-2，引入通道级的擦除门（b_t）和写入门（w_t），分别独立控制旧内容的擦除和新内容的提交。该方法可简化为KDA（当两门退化为同一标量时）和Gated DeltaNet（当衰减进一步退化时）。还推导了快速权重更新视角、分块WY算法（含通道级衰减的非对称擦除因子）以及门控感知的反向传播。

Result: 在1.3B参数、100B FineWeb-Edu tokens上训练，Gated DeltaNet-2在语言建模、常识推理和检索任务上全面优于Mamba-2、Gated DeltaNet、KDA和Mamba-3。在长上下文RULER needle-in-a-haystack基准上优势尤为显著，多键检索设置中表现突出。

Conclusion: 解耦线性注意力中的擦除和写入操作能有效提升记忆编辑的灵活性和模型性能，尤其在长上下文场景下。代码已开源。

Abstract: Linear attention replaces the unbounded cache of softmax attention with a fixed-size recurrent state, reducing sequence mixing to linear time and decoding to constant memory. The hard part is not just what to forget, but how to edit this compressed memory without scrambling existing associations. Delta-rule models subtract the current read before writing a new value, and Kimi Delta Attention (KDA) sharpens forgetting with channel-wise decay. But the active edit still uses a single scalar gate to control two different things: how much old content to erase on the key side and how much new content to commit on the value side. We introduce Gated DeltaNet-2, which generalizes both Gated DeltaNet and KDA by inheriting adaptive forgetting and channel-wise decay while addressing their shared limitation, the scalar tie between erasing and writing. Gated Delta Rule-2 separates these roles with a channel-wise erase gate b_t and a channel-wise write gate w_t, reducing to KDA when both gates collapse to the same scalar and to Gated DeltaNet when the decay also collapses. We derive a fast-weight update view, a chunkwise WY algorithm with channel-wise decay absorbed into asymmetric erase factors, and a gate-aware backward pass that preserves efficient parallel training. At 1.3B parameters trained on 100B FineWeb-Edu tokens, Gated DeltaNet-2 achieves the strongest overall results among Mamba-2, Gated DeltaNet, KDA, and Mamba-3 variants across language modeling, commonsense reasoning, and retrieval. Its advantage is most pronounced on long-context RULER needle-in-a-haystack benchmarks, where it improves the evaluated multi-key retrieval setting and remains strong in both recurrent and hybrid settings. Code is available at https://github.com/NVlabs/GatedDeltaNet-2.

</details>


### [256] [MOSS: Self-Evolution through Source-Level Rewriting in Autonomous Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22794)
*Qianshu Cai,Yonggang Zhang,Xianzhang Jia,Wei Xue,Jun Song,Xinmei Tian,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MOSS 是一个在源码级别实现自主智能体系统自我演化的框架，通过自动收集生产故障证据、确定性多阶段代码修改流水线、批量回放验证和容器热替换机制，使智能体能够在不依赖人类干预的情况下修复自身结构性缺陷。在 OpenClaw 基准测试上，单周期内将平均评分从 0.25 提升至 0.61。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主智能体系统部署后无法从用户交互中学习，持续性故障只能等待人工更新修复。已有的自演化智能体仅能在文本可变工件（如技能文件、提示配置、记忆模式、工作流图）层面演化，无法触及位于代码层的路由、钩子排序、状态不变量和调度等结构性问题，因此存在一类物理上不可达的结构性故障。

Method: MOSS 采用源码级自我重写机制：1) 自动批量收集生产故障证据；2) 通过确定性多阶段流水线进行处理，代码修改委托给可插拔的外部编码智能体 CLI，MOSS 保留阶段排序和裁决权；3) 在临时试验工作器中通过回放故障批次验证候选修改；4) 通过用户同意门控的就地容器热替换和健康探针门控的回滚机制进行部署。

Result: 在 OpenClaw 基准测试上，MOSS 在单周期内无需人工干预即将四项任务的平均评分从 0.25 提升至 0.61。

Conclusion: 源码级自适应是一种比文本层演化更通用的媒介：它是图灵完备的，是任何文本可变作用域的严格超集，能够确定性生效而不依赖基座模型合规性，且不会因长上下文漂移而退化。

Abstract: Autonomous agentic systems are largely static after deployment: they do not learn from user interactions, and recurring failures persist until the next human-driven update ships a fix. Self-evolving agents have emerged in response, but all confine evolution to text-mutable artifacts -- skill files, prompt configurations, memory schemas, workflow graphs -- and leave the agent harness untouched. Since routing, hook ordering, state invariants, and dispatch live in code rather than in any text artifact, an entire class of structural failure is physically unreachable from the text layer. We argue that source-level adaptation is a fundamentally more general medium: it is Turing-complete, a strict superset of every text-mutable scope, takes effect deterministically rather than through base-model compliance, and does not erode under long-context drift. We present MOSS, a system that performs self-rewriting at the source level on production agentic substrates. Each evolution is anchored to an automatically curated batch of production-failure evidence and proceeds through a deterministic multi-stage pipeline; code modification is delegated to a pluggable external coding-agent CLI while MOSS retains stage ordering and verdicts. Candidates are verified by replaying the batch against the candidate image in ephemeral trial workers, then promoted via user-consent-gated, in-place container swap with health-probe-gated rollback. On OpenClaw, MOSS lifts a four-task mean grader score from 0.25 to 0.61 in a single cycle without human intervention.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [257] [Adaptive RBF-KAN: A Comparative Evaluation of Dynamic Shape Parameters in Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21534)
*Roberto Cavoretto,Alessandra De Rossi,Adeeba Haider,Amir Noorizadegan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应径向基函数核的KAN变体（Adaptive RBF-KAN），通过引入Matérn和Wendland核族，并利用留一交叉验证（LOOCV）初始化核形状参数，在训练中自适应调整，在二维基准函数上验证了不同核对于平滑、间断和振荡模式的性能优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KAN使用B样条作为边缘函数计算开销大；FastKAN用高斯RBF改进效率，但核函数固定且形状参数不可调，限制了灵活性和适应性。

Method: 1) 引入Matérn核和Wendland核到KAN框架中；2) 使用LOOCV对核形状参数进行数据驱动的初始化；3) 在训练过程中进一步优化该形状参数；4) 在多个二维基准函数上评估不同核的表现。

Result: 不同核函数对平滑函数、间断函数和振荡模式各有优势；LOOCV初始化结合自适应核学习能有效提升RBF-KAN模型的性能。

Conclusion: 将LOOCV初始化与自适应核学习相结合是改进RBF基KAN模型的实用策略，拓展了KAN的核函数选择空间。

Abstract: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) approximate multivariate functions using learnable univariate edge functions, typically parameterized by B-spline bases. Although effective, spline-based implementations can be computationally expensive. A modified version of KANs, called FastKAN, improves efficiency by replacing splines with Gaussian radial basis functions (RBFs), but it relies on a fixed kernel and shape parameter. In this work, we extend the RBF-based KAN framework by introducing a broader family of radial basis kernels and by initializing the kernel shape parameter using leave-one-out cross-validation (LOOCV). To the best of our knowledge, this is the first study that integrates LOOCV-based kernel scale estimation with deep KAN training. We also introduce Matérn and Wendland kernels into the KAN framework for the first time, enabling more flexible basis representations beyond the Gaussian kernel used in FastKAN. The LOOCV estimate provides a data-driven initialization of the kernel scale, which is subsequently refined during network training. The proposed adaptive RBF-KAN is evaluated on several two-dimensional benchmark functions. The results highlight the importance of kernel selection and adaptive shape parameters, with different kernels showing advantages for smooth functions, discontinuities, and oscillatory patterns. Overall, combining LOOCV-based initialization with adaptive kernel learning provides a practical strategy for improving RBF-based KAN models.

</details>


### [258] [Local Covariate Selection for Average Causal Effect Estimation without Pretreatment and Causal Sufficiency Assumptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21548)
*Zeyu Liu,Zheng Li,Feng Xie,Yan Zeng,Hao Zhang,Kun Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种局部学习方法来选择协变量以进行无偏因果效应估计，避免了预处理假设和因果充分性假设，在非参数因果效应估计中实现了高效且准确的协变量选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协变量选择方法要么依赖全局因果结构学习（在高维场景下计算代价高昂），要么依赖强假设（如因果充分性假设或预处理假设），这些假设在实践中往往不切实际。因此需要一种既能避免这些假设又能高效计算的方法。

Method: 首先刻画一个局部边界，该边界包含至少一个有效调整集（当存在用于识别因果效应的调整集时）；然后开发局部识别程序在该边界内高效搜索。该方法不依赖全局结构学习，也不要求预处理或因果充分性假设。

Result: 在多个合成数据集和两个真实数据集上的实验表明，该方法在实现准确因果效应估计的同时，大幅提升了计算效率。理论分析证明了该方法的可靠性和完备性。

Conclusion: 提出的局部学习方法能有效解决高维场景下协变量选择的计算瓶颈问题，同时放宽了传统方法的强假设限制，为非参数因果效应估计提供了一种实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: We study the problem of selecting covariates for unbiased estimation of the total causal effect.Existing approaches typically rely on global causal structure learning over all variables, or on strong assumptions such as causal sufficiency - where observed variables share no latent confounders - or the pretreatment assumption, which limits covariates to those unaffected by the treatment or outcome. These requirements are often unrealistic in practice, and global learning becomes computationally prohibitive in high-dimensional settings.To address these challenges, we propose a novel local learning method for covariate selection in nonparametric causal effect estimation that avoids both the pretreatment and causal sufficiency assumptions. We first characterize a local boundary that contains at least one valid adjustment set whenever one exists for identifying the causal effect, and then develop local identification procedures to efficiently search within this boundary.We prove that the proposed method is sound and complete. Experiments on multiple synthetic datasets and two real-world datasets show that our approach achieves accurate causal effect estimation while substantially improving computational efficiency.

</details>


### [259] [Scalable On-Policy Reinforcement Learning via Adaptive Batch Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21557)
*Jongchan Park*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应批次缩放（ABS）方法，通过行为散度指标动态调整强化学习中的批次大小，打破了大批次训练在RL中表现不佳的传统认知，在ALE基准上取得了优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为大批次训练与强化学习不兼容，因为RL中数据分布的非平稳性会导致大批次训练性能下降。本文挑战这一观点，观察到非平稳性并非RL的固定属性，而是随训练过程演变：早期阶段需要小批次以保持塑性，后期接近准平稳状态时可使用大批次实现精确收敛。

Method: 提出自适应批次缩放（ABS）方法，核心是行为散度（Behavioral Divergence）——一种通过衡量连续更新间动作级偏移来量化策略非平稳性的新指标。ABS根据策略稳定性动态调整有效批次大小，批次大小与策略波动性成反比。将ABS集成到并行Q网络（PQN）算法中，在ALE基准上进行评估。

Result: 结果表明，与常规认知相反，更大网络与更大批次的组合实现了最佳性能，这种此前被认为在RL中无法实现的扩展行为，现在通过自适应批次控制得以解锁。

Conclusion: ABS方法有效地调和了RL训练中早期塑性需求与后期稳定收敛之间的矛盾，通过动态批次调整实现了以往被认为不可行的规模扩展行为。

Abstract: Conventional wisdom holds that large-batch training is fundamentally incompatible with Reinforcement Learning (RL) - beyond a modest threshold, increasing batch sizes typically yields diminishing returns or performance degradation due to the inherent non-stationarity of the data distribution. We challenge this view by observing that non-stationarity is not a fixed property of RL, but evolves throughout training: early stages exhibit rapid behavioral shifts that demand small batches for plasticity, whereas late stages approach a quasi-stationary regime where large batches enable precise convergence. Motivated by this observation, we propose Adaptive Batch Scaling (ABS), that dynamically adjusts the effective batch size according to the stability of the learning policy. Central to ABS is Behavioral Divergence, a novel metric that quantifies policy non-stationarity by measuring action-level shifts between consecutive updates, which we use to scale batch size inversely to policy volatility. Integrated with the Parallelised Q-Network (PQN) algorithm and evaluated on the ALE benchmark, ABS seamlessly reconciles early-stage plasticity with late-stage stable convergence. Strikingly, contrary to conventional wisdom, our results reveal that the combination of larger networks and larger batch sizes achieves the best performance - a scaling behavior previously thought to be unattainable in RL, now unlocked through adaptive batch control.

</details>


### [260] [Support-aware offline policy selection for advertising marketplaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21736)
*Prashant Shekhar,Caroline Howard*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种支持感知的离线决策框架，用于广告拍卖中的底价政策选择。该框架将日志证据转化为保守的决策对象（包括已认证政策、统计支配选项和待验证候选者），而非单一的点估计排序。理论贡献包括统一的有穷目录保证、支持局部化回放泛化、信息论阈值分辨率限制及异质出价者响应分析。实验表明，该框架能将19个政策缩减为2个验证候选，同时在44个细分市场中确保非伤害性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线底价评估方法（如回放表和离策略评估）仅能估计或排序政策价值，无法直接回答"现有证据是否足够证明政策值得验证"这一操作性问题。这些方法存在弱阈值支持、多重比较效应、子群伤害和出价者响应不确定性等风险，因此需要一种能输出保守验证决策（而非点估计排名）的框架。

Method: 提出支持感知的离线决策框架：将日志证据转化为三类决策对象（已认证政策、统计支配选项、待验证候选者）。理论部分：（1）统一有穷目录保证，在同步不确定性控制和保守支持门控下保留最优通过政策；（2）支持局部化回放泛化分析；（3）信息论阈值分辨率极限；（4）异质出价者响应对局部化回放排名的颠覆量化。实验采用iPinYou实时竞价日志数据。

Result: 在iPinYou数据上：领先底价规则在第二季实现47.66%回放提升、40.71%同步下界提升，第三季实现43.87%冻结超时回放提升。框架将19个政策目录缩减为2个政策的验证短名单，在44个广告主、交易所和区域细分市场中认证非伤害性。

Conclusion: 离线底价政策评估应产生认证后的验证决策，而非仅输出点估计排名。该框架的核心主张得到实验支持。

Abstract: Logged advertising auctions make offline reserve-price evaluation attractive but risky. Replay tables can identify policies with large apparent yield gains, yet they can also hide weak threshold support, multiple-comparison effects, subgroup harm, and bidder-response uncertainty. Existing replay and off-policy evaluation methods estimate or rank policy values, but they do not directly answer the operational question of whether the available evidence is strong enough to justify validation. This paper develops a support-aware offline decision framework for reserve-policy selection. Rather than outputting a single point-estimate winner, the framework converts logged evidence into a conservative decision object consisting of certified policies, statistically dominated alternatives, and unresolved candidates requiring further validation. The main theoretical result gives a unified finite-catalog guarantee showing that, under simultaneous uncertainty control and conservative support gates, the framework preserves the best gate-passing policy while eliminating only policies with certified regret. Supporting results characterize support-localized replay generalization, establish information-theoretic threshold-resolution limits, and quantify when heterogeneous bidder response can overturn localized replay rankings. Experiments on iPinYou real-time-bidding logs show that the leading reserve rule achieves a 47.66% replay lift in season two, a 40.71% simultaneous lower-bound lift, and a 43.87% frozen out-of-time replay lift in season three. The framework reduces a 19-policy catalog to a two-policy validation shortlist while certifying non-harm across 44 advertiser, exchange, and region segments. The results support the central claim that offline reserve-policy evaluation should produce certified validation decisions rather than point-estimate rankings alone.

</details>


### [261] [Uniform-in-Time Weak Propagation-of-Chaos in Shallow Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22010)
*Margalit Glasgow,Joan Bruna*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文建立了在特征学习机制下训练的有限宽度单隐层神经网络与其无限宽度平均场极限之间的弱传播混沌（weak propagation-of-chaos）的非渐近界，该界在时间上一致成立。通过利用平均场确定性Wasserstein梯度流动力学的收敛速率，在满足$\int_0^\infty L_t^{1/2} dt =O(\log d)$的条件下，得到了$\|f_{ρ_t^{MF}}- f_{\hatρ_t^m}\|^2 \lesssim \text{poly}(d) m^{-\min(1,c/6)}$的均匀时间界。该结果不依赖于损失景观的（局部）强凸性或者对数Sobolev不等式，且可推广到样本离散化和时间离散化情形。关键结论是：当平均场群体损失动力学收敛速率快于$t^{-2}$时，仅需$\text{poly}(d/ε)$个神经元、训练样本和梯度下降步即可达到$ε$损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作虽然能通过Grönwall估计获得有限宽度网络与无限宽度平均场极限之间在常数时间上的界，但长时间行为（uniform-in-time bound）的分析更为困难。已有的均匀时间界通常依赖于损失景观的（局部）强凸性或随机梯度动力学中的对数Sobolev不等式。本文旨在不依赖这些几何假设的情况下，建立非渐近且时间一致成立的弱传播混沌界。

Method: 利用平均场确定性Wasserstein梯度流动力学的收敛速率来替代传统的强凸性或LSI条件。具体而言，记$L_t$为平均场超额MSE损失，在标准正则性假设及$\int_0^\infty L_t^{1/2} dt =O(\log d)$的条件下，通过分析损失衰减速率$L_t \lesssim t^{-c}$与波动界之间的关系，导出均匀时间界$\|f_{ρ_t^{MF}}- f_{\hatρ_t^m}\|^2 \lesssim \text{poly}(d) m^{-\min(1,c/6)}$。

Result: 建立了非渐近的、时间一致成立的弱传播混沌界：当平均场损失以$t^{-c}$速率衰减时，有限宽度网络与无限宽度极限之间的误差以$m^{-\min(1,c/6)}$的量级随神经元数$m$增加而衰减。特别地，当$c>2$（即损失衰减快于$t^{-2}$）时，仅需$\text{poly}(d/ε)$个神经元、样本和梯度下降步即可达到$ε$的损失。该结果适用于无噪声环境，不依赖损失景观的几何性质，并可推广到样本离散化和时间离散化。

Conclusion: 本文通过利用平均场动力学的收敛速率，成功绕过了传统均匀时间界对强凸性或LSI的依赖，为特征学习机制下训练神经网络的有限宽度近似提供了更普适的理论保证。该结果揭示了平均场损失收敛速率与所需神经元数量之间的定量关系，对理解神经网络的宽度与训练效率之间的权衡具有重要意义。

Abstract: We consider one-hidden layer neural networks trained in the feature-learning regime using gradient descent, and relate the output of the finite-width network $f_{\hatρ_t^m}$ to its infinite-width counterpart $f_{ρ_t^{MF}}$, which evolves in the mean-field dynamics.
  While constant-time horizon bounds for $\|f_{ρ_t^{MF}} - f_{\hatρ_t^m}\|$ may be obtained via standard Grönwall estimates, the long-time behavior of the fluctuation is a more delicate matter. Uniform-in-time bounds often rely on (local) strong convexity in the landscape or Logarithmic Sobolev inequalities present in noisy gradient dynamics.
  In this work, we establish non-asymptotic weak propagation-of-chaos that holds uniformly in time, obtained by exploiting instead the convergence rate of the mean-field deterministic Wasserstein-gradient-flow dynamics. Specifically, denoting by $L_t$ the mean-field excess MSE loss at time $t$ and $m$ the number of neurons, under standard regularity assumptions and the condition $\int_0^\infty L_t^{1/2} dt =O(\log d)$, we obtain the uniform in time bound $\|f_{ρ_t^{MF}}- f_{\hatρ_t^m}\|^2 \lesssim \text{poly}(d) m^{-\min(1,c/6)}$ whenever $L_t \lesssim t^{-c}$. Our result holds in a noiseless setting and does not make any assumptions on the geometry of the landscape near the optimum, and extends seamlessly to other forms of discretization, including finite number of samples and time discretization. A key takeaway of our result is that whenever the convergence rate of the mean-field, population-loss dynamics is faster than $t^{-2}$, we can attain a loss of $ε$ with only $\text{poly}(d/ε)$ neurons, training samples, and GD steps.

</details>


### [262] [From Betting to Empirical Bernstein LIL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22124)
*Francesco Orabona*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文是作者2017-2018年间撰写的一份技术报告，旨在利用在线投注策略的财富保证来推导迭代对数定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望展示如何通过在线投注策略的财富保证（即一个赌徒在公平赌博中的财富增长界限）来推导概率论中的经典结果——迭代对数定律，从而建立博弈论与经典概率论之间的联系。

Method: 利用在线投注策略的财富保证（即赌徒财富的上下界），通过分析财富增长过程的波动性和极限行为，推导迭代对数定律。

Result: 成功使用在线投注策略的财富保证推导出迭代对数定律，提供了一种不同于传统测度论方法的证明路径。

Conclusion: 迭代对数定律可以通过博弈论框架下的在线投注策略财富保证来推导，这展示了概率论中经典结论的另一种构造性证明方法。

Abstract: This is a verbatim copy of a technical report I wrote in 2017-2018 to obtain the law of the iterated logarithm using the guarantee on the wealth of an online betting strategy.

</details>


### [263] [Departure from Regularity: Degree Heterogeneity and Eigengap as the Structural Drivers of ASE-LSE Latent Subspace Disagreement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22346)
*Minh Triet Pham,Ian Gallagher*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了邻接谱嵌入与拉普拉斯谱嵌入在图上产生不同结果的结构性原因，发现正则性是两者完全一致的充分条件，度异质性和社区结构强度分别推动和抑制两者之间的分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 邻接谱嵌入（ASE）与拉普拉斯谱嵌入（LSE）是图数据分析中最常用的两种谱嵌入方法，但它们在同一个网络上往往产生不同的结果，而背后的结构性原因尚未被完全理解，本文旨在从图结构角度解释这一分歧。

Method: 理论证明当每个节点具有相同连接数（正则性）时两种嵌入完全一致；通过推导显式界，将分歧归因于两个结构性因素：度异质性（推动分歧）和社区结构强度（抑制分歧）；并在数千个模拟网络上进行实证验证。

Result: 正则性是ASE与LSE完全一致的充分条件；度异质性增加两者分歧，社区结构强度抑制分歧；两者的比值是预测两种嵌入何时可互换的强预测因子。

Conclusion: ASE与LSE之间的分歧可被系统性地理解为度异质性与社区结构强度之间的权衡，这为实践中选择何种嵌入方法提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Two of the most widely used methods for analysing graph data, Adjacency Spectral Embedding and Laplacian Spectral Embedding, often produce different results when applied to the same network. Yet the structural reasons behind this disagreement remain incompletely understood. This paper provides a structural account. We show that regularity is a sufficient condition for perfect agreement: when every node has the same number of connections, the two methods produce identical latent subspaces. Any departure from this regularity introduces disagreement, and we prove an explicit bound whose two terms suggest the structural ingredients controlling it: degree heterogeneity, which pushes the methods apart, and community structure strength, which pulls them back together. We validate both drivers empirically across thousands of simulated networks, confirming that heterogeneity drives disagreement up, community strength suppresses it, and their ratio provides a strong predictor of when the two embeddings can be treated as interchangeable and when they cannot.

</details>


### [264] [Do Not Trust The Auctioneer: Learning to Bid in Feedback-Manipulated Auctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22438)
*Luigi Foscari,Matilde Tullii,Vianney Perchet*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究重复一价拍卖中，虚假投标（shilling）仅影响反馈而不影响分配的场景。学习者在输掉拍卖后观察到的是真实投标与虚假投标的最大值，而非真实投标本身。我们提出了一种结合鲁棒区间消除分支（实现$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{2/3})$遗憾）和乐观去偏分支（实现$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{T})$遗憾）的算法，并通过验证与竞赛程序自适应选择分支，获得了与下界匹配的最优遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚假投标（shilling）是拍卖中常见的不诚实行为，它通过人工抬价使竞争显得更激烈。虽然现有工作关注shilling对分配结果的影响，但本文研究的是shilling仅改变反馈信息而不改变分配结果的场景，即学习者在输掉时看到的不是真实出价而是真实出价与虚假出价中的最大值。这种反馈层面的操纵如何影响学习者的学习过程是尚未被探索的问题。

Method: 算法包含两个分支：1) 鲁棒区间消除分支：忽略被污染的输方报告，仅利用赢方信息，达到动态定价问题的$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{2/3})$遗憾率；2) 乐观去偏分支：对输方报告进行去偏处理，利用可靠的尾随信息（suffix information），达到一价拍卖的$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{T})$遗憾率。通过验证与竞赛（validation and racing）程序，算法无需事先知道正确尺度或反馈几何结构即可自适应选择使用乐观更新。

Result: 上界：鲁棒分支实现$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{2/3})$遗憾，乐观分支实现$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{T})$遗憾。下界：在单活跃区域（single-active-region）情况下，提供了与上界匹配（对数因子内）的下界。总体表明，即使仅影响反馈的虚假投标也会显著改变重复投标的统计难度。

Conclusion: 反馈层面的虚假投标操纵可以根本性地改变重复拍卖学习的统计困难度。即使虚假投标分布已知，其存在也会掩蔽真实出价信息，使得学习变得更加困难。本文的算法能够在不知道问题参数的条件下自适应地在两种策略间切换，达到最优的遗憾保证。

Abstract: Shilling is the use of artificial bids to make competition appear stronger and push prices upward. We study repeated first-price auctions in which shilling affects feedback but not allocation: the learner wins or loses against the real competing bid, but after a loss observes the maximum of the real bid and an independent shill bid. Thus the manipulation changes what the learner observes and hence how it learns to bid, without changing the outcome of the current auction. We analyze regret with respect to the best bid benchmark, assuming that the shill-bid distribution is known. Even then, shilling can mask the real bid, while useful side information appears only through intermittent low-shill events. Our algorithm combines a robust interval-elimination branch, which ignores the shilled report and achieves the dynamic-pricing rate $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{2/3})$, with an optimistic branch that debiases losing-side reports and exploits the resulting suffix information when it is reliable and achieves the first-price auctions rate $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{T})$. A validation and racing procedure lets the algorithm use these optimistic updates without knowing the right scale or feedback geometry in advance. We complement the upper bounds with a matching lower bound, up to logarithmic factors, in the single-active-region case. Overall, the results show that even feedback-only shilling can sharply alter the statistical difficulty of repeated bidding.

</details>


### [265] [A Martingale Kernel Independence Test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22549)
*Felix Laumann,Zhaolu Liu,Mauricio Barahona*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出两种新的标准化统计量 mHSIC 和 mdHSIC，分别用于独立性和联合独立性检验。它们通过学生化构造使原假设下的零分布为标准正态，从而用单次正态分位数查询替代传统的置换校准，大幅降低计算成本。mHSIC 不依赖样本分割，计算复杂度为 O(n²)；mdHSIC 通过半样本分割实现有限样本一致性，计算复杂度在联合变量数 d 上呈线性。实验表明，两者在保持与置换基线相当的统计功效和第一类错误率的同时，提速 25-60 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HSIC 和 dHSIC 作为退化的 V 统计量，其零分布依赖于数据的加权 χ² 分布，实践中必须通过置换校准来获取 p 值，这导致每次检验的计算开销被置换次数（通常为数百次）倍增，计算效率低下。

Method: 1) 借鉴近期双样本检验中的 martingale MMD 构造，提出自标准化的 mHSIC 统计量，基于下三角哈达玛积的自标准化和；2) 提出 mdHSIC，通过半样本分割先估计中心化，再在另一半数据上运行下三角自标准化鞅，使条件均值残差随 d 指数衰减；3) 理论上证明在原假设下两者渐近服从标准正态分布，且在固定备择下具有一致性。

Result: 在合成数据实验（每变量维度 1-500，联合检验变量数 2-10）中，mHSIC 和 mdHSIC 的经验第一类错误率和检验功效均与置换校准基线相当，运行速度提升 25-60 倍。

Conclusion: 所提方法成功消除了 HSIC 类检验中置换校准的计算瓶颈，通过学生化构造实现标准正态极限分布，在保持统计性能的同时大幅提升计算效率，尤其在高维和多重检验场景下具有显著优势。

Abstract: The Hilbert-Schmidt Independence Criterion (HSIC) and its joint-independence extension $d\mathrm{HSIC}$ are degenerate $V$-statistics whose data-dependent weighted-$χ^2$ null limits force a permutation calibration that multiplies the per-test cost by the number of permutations, in practice two orders of magnitude. Adapting the recent martingale MMD construction for two-sample testing to the (joint) independence problem, we introduce two studentised statistics whose null distributions are standard normal regardless of the data law, so that a single normal-quantile lookup replaces the permutation step entirely. The first, $m\mathrm{HSIC}$, is a self-normalised lower-triangular sum of the Hadamard product of two empirically centred Gram matrices. Under independence and bounded-fourth-moment kernels it converges to a standard normal. It is consistent against every fixed alternative, and runs at quadratic cost in the sample size without any sample split, matching the biased HSIC $V$-statistic. Our second statistic, $md\mathrm{HSIC}$, achieves finite-sample consistency with a single half-sample split: the centring is estimated on one half and the lower-triangular self-normalised martingale is run on the other, shrinking the conditional-mean residual to a quantity that is exponentially small in $d$, so the statistic is asymptotically standard normal at every fixed number of jointly tested variables, with a per-test cost that grows only linearly in $d$. On synthetic data with per-variable input dimension from $1$ to $500$ and between $2$ and $10$ jointly tested variables, both statistics match the empirical type-I error rate and test power of permutation-calibrated baselines while running $25$ to $60\times$ faster.

</details>


### [266] [Finite-Particle Convergence Rates for Conservative and Non-Conservative Drifting Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22795)
*Krishnakumar Balasubramanian*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一种用于一步生成建模的保守漂移方法，该方法用KDE梯度速度替代原始位移漂移速度，解决了非保守性问题，并给出了连续时间有限粒子的收敛界，推导出残差速度率，同时分析了非保守漂移方法（Laplace核）的性能，最后将残差速度界转化为一步生成保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决一般位移漂移场存在的非保守性问题，提供一种保守的漂移方法用于一步生成建模。

Method: 用核密度估计（KDE）梯度速度（核平滑数据得分与核平滑模型得分之差）替代原始位移漂移速度，构成保守梯度场；通过联合熵恒等式推导经验Stein漂移、KDE平滑Fisher散度和中心速度平方的有限粒子收敛界；分析了不同核函数（包括Laplace核）下的收敛行为。

Result: 在h均匀正交规则条件下，根残差速度率为N^{-1/(d+4)}；在更一般的增长条件下，优化根率为N^{-(2-β)/(2(d+4-β))}（0≤β<2）；Laplace核的非保守方法存在不可避免的残差项；给出了连续时间残差速度界到一步生成保证的转化机制。

Conclusion: 保守漂移方法有效解决了非保守性问题，通过KDE梯度速度实现了可控的有限粒子收敛界，并可通过漂移大小η将理论界转化为实际的一步生成保证。

Abstract: We propose and analyze a conservative drifting method for one-step generative modeling. The method replaces the original displacement-based drifting velocity by a kernel density estimator (KDE)-gradient velocity, namely the difference of the kernel-smoothed data score and the kernel-smoothed model score. This velocity is a gradient field, addressing the non-conservatism issue identified for general displacement-based drifting fields. We prove continuous-time finite-particle convergence bounds for the conservative method on $\R^d$: a joint-entropy identity yields bounds for the empirical Stein drift, the smoothed Fisher discrepancy of the KDE, and the squared center velocity. The main finite-particle correction is a reciprocal-KDE self-interaction term, and we give deterministic and high-probability local-occupancy conditions under which this term is controlled. We keep the quadrature constants explicit and track their possible bandwidth dependence: the root residual-velocity rate $N^{-1/(d+4)}$ holds under an additional $h$-uniform quadrature regularity condition, while a more general growth condition yields the optimized root rate $N^{-(2-β)/(2(d+4-β))}$, where $0\le β<2$. We also analyze the non-conservative drifting method with Laplace kernel, corresponding to the original displacement-based velocity proposed in~\cite{deng2026drifting}. For this method, a sharp companion kernel decomposes the velocity into a positive scalar preconditioning of a sharp-score mismatch plus a Laplace scale-mismatch residual, producing an analogous finite-particle rate with an unavoidable residual term. Finally, we explain how the continuous-time residual-velocity bounds translate into one-step generation guarantees through the explicit drift size $η$.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [267] [Achieving Directional-Stationarity from a Single Random Direction Step](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22045)
*Dan Greenstein,Nadav Hallak*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出在一般非光滑非凸优化中，通过一个随机方向探索步骤，即可使任意基础优化方法的聚点几乎必然达到方向驻点（d-stationary），并保留原方法的收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在约束非光滑非凸优化中，标准方法通常只能保证弱平稳性，而实现方向驻点（d-stationarity）这一更强的最优性条件一直是一个难题。

Method: 提出一种随机方向探索步骤：在基础优化方法的基础上，增加一次随机采样方向和步长的探索，并基于函数值比较接受候选点。该步骤独立于底层方法的行为。

Result: 所有聚点几乎必然达到d-stationary；对于DCA和prox-linear类方法，探索步骤不破坏原始收敛速率；数值实验验证了效果。

Conclusion: 随机方向探索是一种简单且通用的方法，可在温和正则性条件下将任意基础优化方法提升至d-stationarity保证，同时保持收敛速率。

Abstract: This paper addresses the challenge of obtaining strong optimality guarantees in constrained nonsmooth nonconvex optimization under mild regularity conditions, namely local Lipschitz continuity and existence and continuity of directional derivatives. While standard methods typically ensure weak stationarity notions, achieving directional (d-)stationarity remains nontrivial. We show that a random direction exploration step is sufficient to attain d-stationarity. The proposed approach augments any base optimization method with a single exploration step that samples a direction and step size and accepts the candidate based on a function value comparison. The resulting scheme guarantees that all accumulation points are d-stationary almost surely, independently of the behavior of the underlying method. Moreover, it preserves convergence rates of the base method, as established for DCA and prox-linear-type schemes. The theoretical results are complemented by numerical experiments illustrating the effect and guarantees of the exploration step.

</details>


### [268] [Online Optimization with Unknown Time-Varying Parameters from Noisy Gradient Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22251)
*Shivanshu Tripathi,Maziar Raissi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了成本函数依赖于未知动态变化潜在参数的在线优化问题，提出了一种结合控制理论工具（高斯-马尔可夫估计、工具变量估计和参数预测）的算法，并给出了期望跟踪误差的理论界和数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际在线优化问题中，成本函数常依赖于无法直接测量且动态未知的时变潜在参数（如系统状态、外部干扰等），现有方法难以同时处理参数不可测和动力学未知的双重挑战。

Method: 1) 利用高斯-马尔可夫估计器从含噪梯度观测中重构潜在参数；2) 采用工具变量估计器识别参数动力学；3) 基于识别出的动力学预测未来参数并计算最优解。

Result: 给出了期望跟踪误差的理论上界，并通过数值实验验证了算法有效性。

Conclusion: 所提方法融合了控制理论中的状态估计、系统辨识和预测控制思想，为处理未知动态潜在参数的在线优化问题提供了一种理论保证的解决方案。

Abstract: We study online optimization problems in which the cost function depends on latent, time-varying parameters that are unmeasurable and governed by unknown dynamics. Specifically, we consider a strongly convex cost function whose linear term evolves according to unknown linear stochastic dynamics, while the algorithm has access only to finite noisy gradient measurements. We propose a solution that uses control theoretic tools to reconstruct the latent parameters from gradient observations using a Gauss-Markov estimator, then identifies the parameter dynamics using an instrumental-variable estimator, and finally forecasts the parameters to compute the future minimizer. We provide a bound on the expected tracking error. We illustrate the effectiveness of our algorithm on a series of numerical examples.

</details>


### [269] [Incentive-Aligned Vehicle-to-Vehicle Energy Trading via Nash-Integrated Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22363)
*Yujin Lin,Yue Yang,Hao Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出Nash-MADDPG算法，将纳什议价解融入多智能体深度确定性策略梯度，用于V2V能源交易。在30天连续运行中，社会福利提升61.6%，交易量提升62.9%，Jain公平指数提升40.1%，并在6-100智能体规模下验证了可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有V2V能源交易方法要么依赖集中式优化存在计算限制，要么缺乏公平性保障，难以协调具有不同充电需求和不确定到达-离开时间的自利电动汽车智能体。

Method: 将纳什议价解(NBS)集成到多智能体深度确定性策略梯度(MADDPG)框架中。纳什议价确定高效的双边定价，纳什引导的价格邻近奖励引导智能体学习向议价最优策略收敛。

Result: 30天连续运行中，社会福利提升61.6%，交易量提升62.9%，Jain公平指数提升40.1%；在6-100智能体规模下验证了可扩展性，定价稳定接近纳什议价基准。

Conclusion: Nash-MADDPG方法在V2V能源交易中同时实现了高效性、公平性和可扩展性，为去中心化电动汽车能源交易提供了有效的激励机制对齐方案。

Abstract: Vehicle-to-vehicle (V2V) energy trading enables decentralized peer-to-peer energy exchange among electric vehicles (EVs), reducing grid dependency while monetizing surplus capacity. However, coordinating self-interested EV agents with diverse charging needs and uncertain arrival-departure schedules remains challenging. Existing approaches either require centralized optimization with computational limitations or lack fairness guarantees. This paper integrates Nash Bargaining Solution into Multi-Agent Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient, namely Nash-MADDPG, for incentive-aligned V2V energy trading. Nash bargaining determines efficient bilateral pricing, while Nash-guided price proximity rewards align agent learning toward bargaining-optimal strategies. Evaluation over 30-day continuous operation demonstrates an improvement of 61.6% in social welfare and 62.9% improvement in trading volume over Double Auction, while achieving superior fairness, such as 40.1% improvement in Jain's index. Testing across 6-100 agents over a 30-day horizon with continuous vehicle turnover confirms scalability across population size and empirically stable pricing near the Nash Bargaining benchmark.

</details>


### [270] [Global Convergence of Control-Based Lagrangian Flows for Non-Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22486)
*Simone Pirrera,Francesco Ripa,Daniele Astolfi,Sophie M. Fosson,Vito Cerone,Diego Regruto*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究基于控制理论拉格朗日方法的等式约束优化连续时间动力学，特别考虑比例积分和反馈线性化控制器引发的动力学，证明了非凸问题在约束流形上满足适当凸性时的全局指数收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有收敛性结果依赖于目标函数的强凸性或有界性假设，本文旨在利用约束诱导的几何结构，放宽这些限制条件。

Method: 利用约束流形的几何结构，对比例积分和反馈线性化控制器引发的动力学进行分析，证明非凸问题在约束流形上满足适当凸性时的全局指数收敛性。

Result: 证明了非凸等式约束优化问题在约束流形上满足适当凸性时，基于控制理论拉格朗日方法的动力学具有全局指数收敛性。

Conclusion: 通过利用几何结构而非强凸性假设，拓展了控制理论拉格朗日方法在等式约束优化中的适用性，为非凸问题提供了收敛保证。

Abstract: This paper studies the flows of continuous-time dynamics for equality-constrained optimization based on control-theoretic Lagrangian methods. In particular, we consider dynamics induced by proportional-integral and feedback linearization controllers, which have been recently proposed as alternatives to primal-dual gradient methods. Unlike existing convergence results, which rely on strong convexity of the objective function or boundedness assumptions, we exploit the geometric structure induced by the constraints. Specifically, we show global exponential convergence for non-convex problems that satisfy a suitable convexity property when restricted to the constraint manifold.

</details>


### [271] [A conditional-gradient-based single-loop augmented Lagrangian method for inequality constrained optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22539)
*Xiaozhou Wang,Ting Kei Pong,Zev Woodstock*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对带多个光滑凸不等式约束的复合凸优化问题，提出了一种将增广拉格朗日方法与条件梯度法相结合的算法，并证明了其收敛率与已知最优复杂度匹配，同时在高斯集指示函数情形下获得了加速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有增广拉格朗日方法在处理带多个光滑凸不等式约束的复合凸优化问题时，需要高效的线性最小化预言机，而条件梯度法正适合此类需求。本文旨在将二者结合，并分析其收敛性能。

Method: 算法每次迭代分为两步：(1) 对增广拉格朗日函数执行一步条件梯度法（使用开环步长或短步长两种规则）；(2) 按经典增广拉格朗日方法更新对偶变量（使用递减对偶步长）。

Result: 在两种步长规则下，算法收敛率均达到该类问题已知最优复杂度。当h为一致凸集指示函数时，获得了加速收敛率。

Conclusion: 该算法有效结合了增广拉格朗日与条件梯度法的优势，在处理带约束的复合凸优化问题上实现了最优复杂度，且在某些特殊结构下可获得加速。

Abstract: We consider the problem of minimizing the sum of a Lipschitz differentiable convex function $f$ and a proper closed convex function $h$ that admits efficient linear minimization oracles, subject to multiple smooth convex inequality constraints. We adapt the classical augmented Lagrangian (AL) method for these problems: in each iteration, our algorithm consists of one step of the conditional gradient (CG) method applied to the AL function, followed by an update of the dual variable as in classical AL methods with a diminishing dual stepsize. We study the convergence rate of our algorithm under two standard stepsize rules for the CG method, namely, an open-loop stepsize and the short stepsize, and obtain a convergence rate that matches the best-known complexity for this class of problems. We also establish accelerated rates when $h$ is the indicator function of a uniformly convex set.

</details>


### [272] [Output regulation via input-output data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22562)
*Andrea Bisoffi,Wenjie Liu,Zhongjie Hu,Claudio De Persis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对MIMO离散时间线性系统，仅利用含未知外生信号噪声的输入输出数据，无需系统模型，通过半定规划设计反馈控制器，渐进消除外生信号对输出的影响，从而解决纯数据驱动的输出调节问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的输出调节问题需要已知系统模型，但在实际应用中系统模型往往未知或难以精确获取。本文旨在从纯输入输出数据出发，无需系统模型知识，解决MIMO线性系统的输出调节问题。

Method: 首先，从含噪输入输出数据中构建辅助系统；然后，对辅助系统设计控制器，该设计对应一个半定规划问题；最后，通过严格分析辅助系统与原系统解之间的关系，将控制器设计迁移回原始系统。

Result: 成功实现了无需系统模型、仅依赖输入输出数据的MIMO线性系统输出调节控制器设计，控制器可通过求解半定规划获得。

Conclusion: 本文证明了纯数据驱动方法可以有效解决MIMO线性系统的输出调节问题，拓展了数据驱动控制理论在输出调节领域的应用。

Abstract: From a multi-input-multi-output (MIMO) discrete-time linear system, we collect input-output data affected by noise in the form of an unknown exosignal and, from these data points (without knowledge of the system model), we design a feedback controller that asymptotically annihilates the effect of that exosignal on the output. This amounts to solving an output regulation problem purely from input-output data, for MIMO linear systems. The design of the controller corresponds to a semidefinite program and is pursued on a suitable auxiliary system. Such design carries over from the auxiliary system to the original one by a rigorous examination of the relation between the solutions of the two systems.

</details>


### [273] [Characterization of Normalizer of Lie Superalgebra and its Application to Control Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22694)
*Aroonima Sahoo,Kishor Chandra Pati,Tofan Kumar Khuntia*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了以李超群为构型空间的、同时包含玻色子和费米子变量的控制系统，通过左不变向量场的李超代数的正规化子来刻画控制系统，并提出了线性控制系统的可控性判据及示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超对称理论中，包含玻色子和费米子变量的动力系统具有重要作用，但这类系统的控制问题尚未得到充分研究，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 利用李超群上所有光滑向量场的李超代数中左不变向量场的李超子代数的正规化子来刻画控制系统，并对线性控制系统进行详细分析。

Result: 提出了线性控制系统的可控性判据，并给出了相应的示例加以说明。

Conclusion: 本文为以李超群为构型空间的玻色-费米子混合控制系统提供了理论框架和可控性分析方法。

Abstract: The dynamical systems having both bosonic and fermionic variables play an important role in the theory of supersymmetry. This article addresses the control problems including both bosonic and fermionic variables on Lie supergroup as the configuration space. Here, the control systems are characterized using the normalizer of Lie subsuperalgebra of left-invariant vector fields in the Lie superalgebra of all smooth vector fields of Lie supergroup. Then, the linear control system is studied in detail and its controllability criterion is proposed along with suitable examples.

</details>


### [274] [Optimization over the intersection of manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22736)
*Yan Yang,Bin Gao,Ya-xiang Yuan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了流形交集中的正则性条件（clean intersection 和 intrinsic transversality）的等价性，并提出了一种仅在一个流形上使用 retraction、沿两个正交方向更新迭代点的几何优化方法，在稀疏与低秩优化等应用中取得了有效结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两个流形交集上的优化问题广泛存在，但可行区域的耦合几何结构使得优化困难，现有方法缺乏高效且理论上保证的求解手段。

Method: 证明 clean intersection 与 intrinsic transversality 的等价性，从而获得到交集切空间的可计算投影；提出仅在一个流形上使用 retraction，迭代点沿两个正交方向更新——一个方向渐近接近另一个流形，另一个方向降低目标函数值。

Result: 在 intrinsic transversality 条件下，推导了可行性和最优性度量的收敛率，证明每个聚点都是一阶稳定点；在球面数据拟合、双曲嵌入、压缩模式等稀疏和低秩优化数值实验中验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的几何方法能够有效处理两个流形交集上的优化问题，理论保证强，数值表现良好。

Abstract: Optimization over the intersection of two manifolds arises in a broad range of applications, but is hindered by the coupled geometry of the feasible region. In this paper, we prove that the regularities -- clean intersection and intrinsic transversality -- are equivalent, which yields a tractable projection onto the tangent space of the intersection. Therefore, we propose a geometric method that employs a retraction on only one manifold and updates the iterate along two orthogonal directions. Specifically, the iterates stay on one manifold, and the two directions are responsible for asymptotically approaching the other manifold and decreasing the objective function, respectively. Under intrinsic transversality, we derive the convergence rate for both the feasibility and optimality measures, and show that every accumulation point is first-order stationary. Numerical experiments on problems stemming from sparse and low-rank optimization, including fitting spherical data, approximating hyperbolic embeddings on real data, and computing compressed modes, demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.
